      if (validation) return formatValidationError(validation);
      const { opId, expectedRevision } = payload;
      if (!validateOpId(opId)) return formatValidationError('Operation 
identifier is missing or malformed.');
      if (!Number.isInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) return 
formatValidationError('Expected revision must be a non-negative integer.');
      return runOperation(accountId, opId, 'competitiveUndo', payload, () => {
        const current = getCompetitiveState(accountId);
        if (!current) return formatNotFoundError('Competitive game not found.');
        if (current.revision !== expectedRevision) {
          return formatConflictError('Competitive game revision is stale.', { 
game: serializeCompetitiveState(current) });
        }
        if (!current.history.length) return formatValidationError('Undo is not 
available at the root.');
        const lastMove = current.history[current.history.length - 1];
        const nextBoard = cloneBoard(current.board);
        nextBoard[lastMove.index] = '';
        current.board = nextBoard;
        current.history = current.history.slice(0, -1);
        current.redoHistory = [lastMove].concat(current.redoHistory);
        current.currentPlayer = lastMove.color;
        current.result = null;
        current.winningCells = [];
        current.revision += 1;
        current.status = 'active';
        const derived = boardResult(current.board);
        if (derived.result) {
          current.result = derived.result;
          current.winningCells = derived.winningCells;
          current.status = 'completed';
        }
        if (lastMove.color === 'Red' && current.redWins > 0 && current.result 
!== 'Red wins') {
          current.redWins -= 1;
        }
        if (lastMove.color === 'Yellow' && current.yellowWins > 0 && 
current.result !== 'Yellow wins') {
          current.yellowWins -= 1;
        }
        if (lastMove.color && current.result !== 'Draw' && current.draws > 0) {
          if (lastMove.color === 'Red' || lastMove.color === 'Yellow') {
            const archive = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM competitive_archive WHERE 
account_id = ? AND round_id = ?').get(accountId, current.roundId);
            if (archive && archive.result === 'Draw') {
              current.draws -= 1;
            }
          }
        }
        maybeUpdateArchiveForCurrentState(accountId, current);
        updateCompetitiveState(accountId, current);
        return { statusCode: 200, body: { game: 
serializeCompetitiveState(current) } };
      });
    }
    
    function redoCompetitiveMove(accountId, payload) {
      const allowedKeys = ['opId', 'expectedRevision'];
      const validation = validateKeysAndValues(payload, allowedKeys);
      if (validation) return formatValidationError(validation);
      const { opId, expectedRevision } = payload;
      if (!validateOpId(opId)) return formatValidationError('Operation 
identifier is missing or malformed.');
      if (!Number.isInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) return 
formatValidationError('Expected revision must be a non-negative integer.');
      return runOperation(accountId, opId, 'competitiveRedo', payload, () => {
        const current = getCompetitiveState(accountId);
        if (!current) return formatNotFoundError('Competitive game not found.');
        if (current.revision !== expectedRevision) {
          return formatConflictError('Competitive game revision is stale.', { 
game: serializeCompetitiveState(current) });
        }
        if (!current.redoHistory.length) return formatValidationError('Redo is 
not available.');
        const redoMove = current.redoHistory[0];
        const rest = current.redoHistory.slice(1);
        const applied = applyMove(current.board, current.history, 
redoMove.column, redoMove.color);
        if (applied.error) return formatValidationError(applied.error);
        current.board = applied.board;
        current.history = current.history.concat([applied.move]);
        current.redoHistory = rest;
        current.currentPlayer = applied.turn;
        current.result = applied.result;
        current.winningCells = applied.winningCells;
        current.revision += 1;
        current.status = applied.result ? 'completed' : 'active';
        if (applied.result === 'Red wins') current.redWins += 1;
        if (applied.result === 'Yellow wins') current.yellowWins += 1;
        if (applied.result === 'Draw') current.draws += 1;
        maybeUpdateArchiveForCurrentState(accountId, current);
        updateCompetitiveState(accountId, current);
        return { statusCode: 200, body: { game: 
serializeCompetitiveState(current) } };
      });
    }
    
    function applyAnalysisCreate(accountId, payload) {
      const allowedKeys = ['opId', 'sourceMatchId', 'sourceStep', 'name'];
      const validation = validateKeysAndValues(payload, allowedKeys);
      if (validation) return formatValidationError(validation);
      const opId = payload.opId;
      if (!validateOpId(opId)) return formatValidationError('Operation 
identifier is missing or malformed.');
      return runOperation(accountId, opId, 'analysisCreate', payload, () => 
createAnalysisFromMatch(accountId, payload));
    }
    
    function applyAnalysisRename(accountId, analysisId, payload) {
      const allowedKeys = ['opId', 'expectedRevision', 'name'];
      const validation = validateKeysAndValues(payload, allowedKeys);
      if (validation) return formatValidationError(validation);
      const { opId, expectedRevision, name } = payload;
      if (!validateOpId(opId)) return formatValidationError('Operation 
identifier is missing or malformed.');
      if (!Number.isInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) return 
formatValidationError('Expected revision must be a non-negative integer.');
      const cleanName = validateName(name);
      if (!cleanName) return formatValidationError('Analysis name must be 1 to 
60 trimmed characters long.');
      return runOperation(accountId, opId, 'analysisRename', payload, () => {
        const stale = maybeConflictWithAnalysis(accountId, analysisId, 
expectedRevision, 'Analysis revision is stale.');
        if (stale) return stale;
        const analysis = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analyses WHERE account_id = ?
AND id = ?').get(accountId, analysisId);
        if (!analysis) return formatNotFoundError('Analysis not found.');
        db.prepare('UPDATE analyses SET name = ?, revision = revision + 1, 
updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?').run(cleanName, nowIso(), analysisId);
        return { statusCode: 200, body: { analysis: getAnalysisById(accountId, 
analysisId).analysis } };
      });
    }
    
    function applyAnalysisSelect(accountId, analysisId, payload) {
      const allowedKeys = ['opId', 'expectedRevision', 'nodeId'];
      const validation = validateKeysAndValues(payload, allowedKeys);
      if (validation) return formatValidationError(validation);
      const { opId, expectedRevision, nodeId } = payload;
      if (!validateOpId(opId)) return formatValidationError('Operation 
identifier is missing or malformed.');
      if (!Number.isInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) return 
formatValidationError('Expected revision must be a non-negative integer.');
      if (!Number.isInteger(nodeId) || nodeId <= 0) return 
formatValidationError('Node identifier must be a positive integer.');
      return runOperation(accountId, opId, 'analysisSelect', payload, () => {
        const stale = maybeConflictWithAnalysis(accountId, analysisId, 
expectedRevision, 'Analysis revision is stale.');
        if (stale) return stale;
        const node = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE id = ? AND 
analysis_id = ?').get(nodeId, analysisId);
        if (!node) return formatNotFoundError('Analysis node not found.');
        db.prepare('UPDATE analyses SET selected_node_id = ?, revision = 
revision + 1, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?').run(nodeId, nowIso(), analysisId);
        return { statusCode: 200, body: buildAnalysisResponse(accountId, 
analysisId) };
      });
    }
    
    function applyAnalysisMove(accountId, analysisId, payload) {
      const allowedKeys = ['opId', 'expectedRevision', 'column'];
      const validation = validateKeysAndValues(payload, allowedKeys);
      if (validation) return formatValidationError(validation);
      const { opId, expectedRevision, column } = payload;
      if (!validateOpId(opId)) return formatValidationError('Operation 
identifier is missing or malformed.');
      if (!Number.isInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) return 
formatValidationError('Expected revision must be a non-negative integer.');
      if (!Number.isInteger(column) || column < 1 || column > BOARD_COLS) return
formatValidationError('Column must be an integer from 1 to 7.');
      return runOperation(accountId, opId, 'analysisMove', payload, () => {
        const stale = maybeConflictWithAnalysis(accountId, analysisId, 
expectedRevision, 'Analysis revision is stale.');
        if (stale) return stale;
        const detail = getAnalysisById(accountId, analysisId);
        if (!detail) return formatNotFoundError('Analysis not found.');
        const selected = detail.nodes.find((node) => node.id === 
detail.analysis.selectedNodeId) || detail.nodes[0];
        if (!selected) return formatConflictError('Analysis has no selected 
node.');
        if (selected.result) return formatValidationError('The selected analysis
node is terminal.');
        const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE 
analysis_id = ? AND parent_id = ? AND incoming_column = ?').get(analysisId, 
selected.id, column);
        let chosenNode;
        if (existing) {
          chosenNode = existing;
        } else {
          const parentHistory = parseHistory(selected.history_json || 
JSON.stringify(selected.history || []));
          const applied = applyMove(selected.board, selected.history, column, 
selected.turn);
          if (applied.error) return formatValidationError(applied.error);
          const inserted = db.prepare(`
            INSERT INTO analysis_nodes (
              analysis_id, parent_id, incoming_column, color, row, cell_index, 
board_json, turn, result, winning_cells_json, history_json
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
          `).run(
            analysisId,
            selected.id,
            column,
            selected.turn,
            applied.move.row,
            applied.move.index,
            JSON.stringify(applied.board),
            applied.turn,
            applied.result,
            JSON.stringify(applied.winningCells),
            JSON.stringify(selected.history.concat([applied.move]))
          );
          chosenNode = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE id = 
?').get(inserted.lastInsertRowid);
        }
        db.prepare('UPDATE analyses SET selected_node_id = ?, revision = 
revision + 1, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?').run(chosenNode.id, nowIso(), 
analysisId);
        return { statusCode: 200, body: buildAnalysisResponse(accountId, 
analysisId) };
      });
    }
    
    function applyAnalysisUndo(accountId, analysisId, payload) {
      const allowedKeys = ['opId', 'expectedRevision'];
      const validation = validateKeysAndValues(payload, allowedKeys);
      if (validation) return formatValidationError(validation);
      const { opId, expectedRevision } = payload;
      if (!validateOpId(opId)) return formatValidationError('Operation 
identifier is missing or malformed.');
      if (!Number.isInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) return 
formatValidationError('Expected revision must be a non-negative integer.');
      return runOperation(accountId, opId, 'analysisUndo', payload, () => {
        const stale = maybeConflictWithAnalysis(accountId, analysisId, 
expectedRevision, 'Analysis revision is stale.');
        if (stale) return stale;
        const detail = getAnalysisById(accountId, analysisId);
        if (!detail) return formatNotFoundError('Analysis not found.');
        const selected = detail.nodes.find((node) => node.id === 
detail.analysis.selectedNodeId) || detail.nodes[0];
        if (!selected || selected.parentId === null || selected.parentId === 
undefined) {
          return formatValidationError('Undo is not available at the source 
root.');
        }
        db.prepare('UPDATE analyses SET selected_node_id = ?, revision = 
revision + 1, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?').run(selected.parentId, nowIso(), 
analysisId);
        return { statusCode: 200, body: buildAnalysisResponse(accountId, 
analysisId) };
      });
    }
    
    function applyAnalysisRedo(accountId, analysisId, payload) {
      const allowedKeys = ['opId', 'expectedRevision', 'childId'];
      const validation = validateKeysAndValues(payload, allowedKeys);
      if (validation) return formatValidationError(validation);
      const { opId, expectedRevision, childId } = payload;
      if (!validateOpId(opId)) return formatValidationError('Operation 
identifier is missing or malformed.');
      if (!Number.isInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) return 
formatValidationError('Expected revision must be a non-negative integer.');
      if (childId !== undefined && (!Number.isInteger(childId) || childId <= 0))
return formatValidationError('Child identifier must be a positive integer when 
provided.');
      return runOperation(accountId, opId, 'analysisRedo', payload, () => {
        const stale = maybeConflictWithAnalysis(accountId, analysisId, 
expectedRevision, 'Analysis revision is stale.');
        if (stale) return stale;
        const detail = getAnalysisById(accountId, analysisId);
        if (!detail) return formatNotFoundError('Analysis not found.');
        const selected = detail.nodes.find((node) => node.id === 
detail.analysis.selectedNodeId) || detail.nodes[0];
        const children = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE 
analysis_id = ? AND parent_id = ? ORDER BY incoming_column ASC, id 
ASC').all(analysisId, selected.id);
        if (!children.length) return formatValidationError('Redo is not 
available.');
        let chosen = null;
        if (children.length === 1 && childId === undefined) {
          chosen = children[0];
        } else if (childId !== undefined) {
          chosen = children.find((child) => child.id === childId) || null;
          if (!chosen) return formatConflictError('The chosen child is not a 
direct continuation of the selected node.');
        } else {
          return formatConflictError('Redo is ambiguous; choose one of the 
available children.', {
            children: children.map((child) => nodeToPublic({
              id: child.id,
              analysisId,
              parentId: child.parent_id,
              incomingColumn: child.incoming_column,
              color: child.color,
              row: child.row,
              cellIndex: child.cell_index,
              board: parseBoard(child.board_json),
              turn: child.turn,
              result: child.result,
              winningCells: parseMaybeJson(child.winning_cells_json, []),
              history: parseHistory(child.history_json),
            })),
          });
        }
        db.prepare('UPDATE analyses SET selected_node_id = ?, revision = 
revision + 1, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?').run(chosen.id, nowIso(), 
analysisId);
        return { statusCode: 200, body: buildAnalysisResponse(accountId, 
analysisId) };
      });
    }
    
    function applyAnalysisCompare(accountId, payload) {
      const allowedKeys = ['analysisId', 'leftNodeId', 'rightNodeId'];
      const validation = validateKeysAndValues(payload, allowedKeys);
      if (validation) return formatValidationError(validation);
      const { analysisId, leftNodeId, rightNodeId } = payload;
      if (![analysisId, leftNodeId, rightNodeId].every((value) => 
Number.isInteger(value) && value > 0)) {
        return formatValidationError('Analysis and node identifiers must be 
positive integers.');
      }
      const comparison = compareNodes(accountId, analysisId, leftNodeId, 
rightNodeId);
      if (!comparison) return formatNotFoundError('Analysis or node not 
found.');
      return { statusCode: 200, body: comparison };
    }
    
    function applyAnalysisReport(accountId, payload) {
      const allowedKeys = ['analysisId', 'nodeId', 'depth'];
      const validation = validateKeysAndValues(payload, allowedKeys);
      if (validation) return formatValidationError(validation);
      const { analysisId, nodeId, depth } = payload;
      if (![analysisId, nodeId].every((value) => Number.isInteger(value) && 
value > 0)) {
        return formatValidationError('Analysis and node identifiers must be 
positive integers.');
      }
      if (!Number.isInteger(depth) || depth < 1 || depth > 4) {
        return formatValidationError('Depth must be an integer from 1 to 4.');
      }
      const detail = getNodeAndAnalysisOr404(accountId, analysisId, nodeId);
      if (!detail) return formatNotFoundError('Analysis node not found.');
      const memo = new Map();
      const node = { ...detail.node, analysisId };
      const boardState = boardResult(node.board);
      const outcome = evaluatePosition(node.board, node.turn, depth, memo);
      const tree = reportTreeForNode(node, depth, memo, []);
      const playableColumns = [];
      if (!boardState.result) {
        for (let column = 1; column <= BOARD_COLS; column += 1) {
          const row = legalDropRow(node.board, column);
          if (!row) continue;
          const applied = applyMove(node.board, node.history, column);
          if (applied.error) continue;
          const childOutcome = evaluatePosition(applied.board, applied.turn, 
depth - 1, memo);
          let label = childOutcome.outcome;
          if (label === 'win' || label === 'loss') label += ` in 
${childOutcome.distance + 1} plies`;
          playableColumns.push({ column, row, outcome: childOutcome.outcome, 
distance: childOutcome.distance, label, board: boardSummary(applied.board) });
        }
      }
      return {
        statusCode: 200,
        body: {
          analysisId,
          nodeId,
          depth,
          perspective: node.turn,
          terminalResult: boardState.result,
          outcome: outcome.outcome,
          distance: outcome.distance,
          winningCells: node.winningCells,
          playableColumns,
          proofTree: tree,
        },
      };
    }
    
    function authResponse(account) {
      const bootstrap = getBootstrap(account.id);
      return { account: { id: account.id, email: account.email, name: 
account.name }, ...bootstrap };
    }
    
    function getBootstrap(accountId) {
      const state = getCompetitiveState(accountId);
      if (!state) return null;
      return {
        game: serializeCompetitiveState(state),
        archive: getArchiveSummaries(accountId),
        analyses: getAnalysisMetadata(accountId),
      };
    }
    
    function loginRoute(req, res) {
      const allowedKeys = ['email', 'password'];
      if (!assertAllowedKeys(req.body, allowedKeys)) {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Request contains unsupported 
fields or is not a JSON object.' });
      }
      const { email, password } = req.body;
      if (typeof email !== 'string' || typeof password !== 'string') {
        return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Email and password are required.' 
});
      }
      const account = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM accounts WHERE email = 
?').get(email.trim());
      if (!account || !verifyPassword(password, account.password_salt, 
account.password_hash)) {
        return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Invalid email or password.' });
      }
      const token = randomToken();
      db.prepare('INSERT INTO sessions (account_id, token_hash, created_at, 
last_seen_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)').run(account.id, sha256(token), nowIso(), 
nowIso());
      return res.json({ token, ...authResponse(account) });
    }
    
    function logoutAllRoute(req, res) {
      const account = requireAccount(req, res);
      if (!account) return;
      db.prepare('UPDATE sessions SET revoked_at = ? WHERE account_id = ? AND 
revoked_at IS NULL').run(nowIso(), account.id);
      res.json({ ok: true });
    }
    
    function bootstrapRoute(req, res) {
      const account = requireAccount(req, res);
      if (!account) return;
      res.json({ tokenOk: true, account: { id: account.id, email: account.email,
name: account.name }, ...getBootstrap(account.id) });
    }
    
    function getArchiveRoute(req, res) {
      const account = requireAccount(req, res);
      if (!account) return;
      const matchId = req.params.matchId;
      const detail = archiveDetail(account.id, matchId);
      if (!detail) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Archive match not 
found.' });
      res.json(detail);
    }
    
    function getAnalysisRoute(req, res) {
      const account = requireAccount(req, res);
      if (!account) return;
      const analysisId = Number(req.params.analysisId);
      if (!Number.isInteger(analysisId) || analysisId <= 0) return 
res.status(400).json({ error: 'Analysis identifier must be a positive integer.' 
});
      const response = buildAnalysisResponse(account.id, analysisId);
      if (!response) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Analysis not found.' 
});
      res.json(response);
    }
    
    function currentAnalysisSelectedNode(accountId, analysisId) {

    seedDatabase();
    
    app.post('/api/auth/login', loginRoute);

+104|      created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
+105|      updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
+106|      FOREIGN KEY (account_id) REFERENCES accounts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
+107|    );
+108|
+109|    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS analysis_nodes (
+110|      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
+111|      analysis_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
+112|      parent_id INTEGER,
+113|      incoming_column INTEGER,
+114|      color TEXT NOT NULL,
+115|      row INTEGER,
+116|      cell_index INTEGER,
+117|      board_json TEXT NOT NULL,
+118|      turn TEXT NOT NULL,
+119|      result TEXT,
+120|      winning_cells_json TEXT NOT NULL,
+121|      history_json TEXT NOT NULL,
+122|      created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
+123|      FOREIGN KEY (analysis_id) REFERENCES analyses(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
+124|      FOREIGN KEY (parent_id) REFERENCES analysis_nodes(id) ON DELETE 
CASCADE
+125|    );
+126|
+127|    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS previews (
+128|      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
+129|      account_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
+130|      source_analysis_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
+131|      source_node_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
+132|      destination_analysis_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
+133|      destination_node_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
+134|      source_revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
+135|      destination_revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
+136|      preview_json TEXT NOT NULL,
+137|      committed INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
+138|      created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
+139|      committed_at TEXT,
+140|      FOREIGN KEY (account_id) REFERENCES accounts(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
+141|    );
+142|
+143|    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sessions_account ON 
sessions(account_id);
+144|    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_archive_account ON 
competitive_archive(account_id, completed_at DESC);
+145|    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_analyses_account ON analyses(account_id,
updated_at DESC);
+146|    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_nodes_analysis ON 
analysis_nodes(analysis_id);
+147|  `);

+149|
+150|function sha256(text) {
+151|  return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(text).digest('hex');
+152|}
+153|
+154|function randomId() {
+155|  return crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
+156|}
+157|
+158|function randomToken() {
+159|  return crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');
+160|}
+161|
+162|function stableStringify(value) {
+163|  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') {
+164|    return JSON.stringify(value);
+165|  }
+166|  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
+167|    return '[' + value.map((item) => stableStringify(item)).join(',') + 
']';
+168|  }
+169|  const keys = Object.keys(value).sort();
+170|  return '{' + keys.map((key) => JSON.stringify(key) + ':' + 
stableStringify(value[key])).join(',') + '}';
+171|}
+172|
+173|function nowIso() {
+174|  return new Date().toISOString();
+175|}
+176|
+177|function isPlainObject(value) {
+178|  return value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
+179|}
+180|
+181|function jsonClone(value) {
+182|  return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
+183|}
+184|
+185|function parseMaybeJson(text, fallback) {
+186|  if (text === null || text === undefined || text === 'none') {
+187|    return fallback;
+188|  }
+189|  if (typeof text === 'string') {
+190|    return JSON.parse(text);
+191|  }
+192|  return fallback;
+193|}
+194|
+195|function createEmptyBoard() {
+196|  return Array.from({ length: TOTAL_CELLS }, () => '');
+197|}
+198|
+199|function cloneBoard(board) {
+200|  return board.slice();
+201|}
+202|
+203|function indexFromRowCol(row, column) {
+204|  return (row - 1) * BOARD_COLS + (column - 1);
+205|}
+206|
+207|function rowColFromIndex(index) {
+208|  return {
+209|    row: Math.floor(index / BOARD_COLS) + 1,
+210|    column: (index % BOARD_COLS) + 1,
+211|  };
+212|}
+213|
+214|function winningCellsForBoard(board) {
+215|  for (let row = 1; row <= BOARD_ROWS; row += 1) {
+216|    for (let column = 1; column <= BOARD_COLS; column += 1) {
+217|      const occupant = board[indexFromRowCol(row, column)];
+218|      if (!occupant) continue;
+219|      const directions = [
+220|        [0, 1],
+221|        [1, 0],
+222|        [1, 1],
+223|        [1, -1],
+224|      ];
+225|      for (const [dr, dc] of directions) {
+226|        const line = [{ row, column }];
+227|        let ok = true;
+228|        for (let step = 1; step < 4; step += 1) {
+229|          const nextRow = row + dr * step;
+230|          const nextColumn = column + dc * step;
+231|          if (nextRow < 1 || nextRow > BOARD_ROWS || nextColumn < 1 || 
nextColumn > BOARD_COLS) {
+232|            ok = false;
+233|            break;
+234|          }
+235|          const nextOccupant = board[indexFromRowCol(nextRow, nextColumn)];
+236|          if (nextOccupant !== occupant) {
+237|            ok = false;
+238|            break;
+239|          }
+240|          line.push({ row: nextRow, column: nextColumn });
+241|        }
+242|        if (ok) {
+243|          return line.map((cell) => indexFromRowCol(cell.row, 
cell.column));
+244|        }
+245|      }
+246|    }
+247|  }
+248|  return [];
+249|}
+250|
+251|function boardResult(board) {
+252|  const winningCells = winningCellsForBoard(board);
+253|  if (winningCells.length) {
+254|    const occupant = board[winningCells[0]];
+255|    return {
+256|      result: `${occupant} wins`,
+257|      winningCells,
+258|    };
+259|  }
+260|  if (board.every((cell) => cell)) {
+261|    return { result: 'Draw', winningCells: [] };
+262|  }
+263|  return { result: null, winningCells: [] };
+264|}
+265|
+266|function currentPlayerAfterHistory(history) {
+267|  return history.length % 2 === 0 ? 'Red' : 'Yellow';
+268|}
+269|
+270|function legalDropRow(board, column) {
+271|  for (let row = BOARD_ROWS; row >= 1; row -= 1) {
+272|    const index = indexFromRowCol(row, column);
+273|    if (!board[index]) return row;
+274|  }
+275|  return null;
+276|}
+277|
+278|function applyMove(board, history, column, forcedColor) {
+279|  if (!Number.isInteger(column) || column < 1 || column > BOARD_COLS) {
+280|    return { error: 'Column must be an integer from 1 to 7.' };
+281|  }
+282|  const currentResult = boardResult(board).result;
+283|  if (currentResult) {
+284|    return { error: 'The position is already terminal.' };
+285|  }
+286|  const row = legalDropRow(board, column);
+287|  if (!row) {
+288|    return { error: `Column ${column} is full.` };
+289|  }
+290|  const color = forcedColor || currentPlayerAfterHistory(history);
+291|  const nextBoard = cloneBoard(board);
+292|  const index = indexFromRowCol(row, column);
+293|  nextBoard[index] = color;
+294|  const resultInfo = boardResult(nextBoard);
+295|  const move = { color, column, row, index };
+296|  return {
+297|    board: nextBoard,
+298|    move,
+299|    result: resultInfo.result,
+300|    winningCells: resultInfo.winningCells,
+301|    turn: resultInfo.result ? color : (color === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red'),
+302|  };
+303|}
+304|
+305|function parseBoard(raw) {
+306|  const board = typeof raw === 'string' ? JSON.parse(raw) : raw;
+307|  if (!Array.isArray(board) || board.length !== TOTAL_CELLS) {
+308|    throw new Error('Invalid board shape');
+309|  }
+310|  return board.map((cell) => (cell === 'Red' || cell === 'Yellow' ? cell : 
''));
+311|}
+312|
+313|function parseHistory(raw) {
+314|  const history = typeof raw === 'string' ? JSON.parse(raw) : raw;
+315|  if (!Array.isArray(history)) {
+316|    throw new Error('Invalid history');
+317|  }
+318|  return history.map((move) => ({
+319|    color: move.color,
+320|    column: move.column,
+321|    row: move.row,
+322|    index: move.index,
+323|  }));
+324|}
+325|
+326|function deriveStateFromHistory(history) {
+327|  let board = createEmptyBoard();
+328|  let result = null;
+329|  let winningCells = [];
+330|  for (const move of history) {
+331|    const applied = applyMove(board, history.slice(0, 
history.indexOf(move)), move.column, move.color);
+332|    if (applied.error) {
+333|      throw new Error(applied.error);
+334|    }
+335|    board = applied.board;
+336|    result = applied.result;
+337|    winningCells = applied.winningCells;
+338|  }
+339|  return {
+340|    board,
+341|    currentPlayer: currentPlayerAfterHistory(history),
+342|    result,
+343|    winningCells,
+344|  };
+345|}

};

};

+357|}

+359|function formatUnauthorizedError(message = 'Unauthorized') {
+360|  return { ok: false, statusCode: 401, body: { error: message } };
+361|}

+363|function validateName(name) {
+364|  if (typeof name !== 'string') return null;
+365|  const trimmed = name.trim();
+366|  if (trimmed.length < 1 || trimmed.length > 60) return null;
+367|  return trimmed;

+372|}

+378|function assertAllowedKeys(body, allowedKeys) {
+379|  if (!isPlainObject(body)) return false;
+380|  return Object.keys(body).every((key) => allowedKeys.includes(key));
+381|}
+382|
+383|function hashPassword(password, salt) {
+384|  return crypto.scryptSync(password, salt, 64).toString('hex');
+385|}
+386|
+387|function verifyPassword(password, salt, expectedHash) {
+388|  const actual = Buffer.from(hashPassword(password, salt), 'hex');
+389|  const expected = Buffer.from(expectedHash, 'hex');
+390|  return actual.length === expected.length && 
crypto.timingSafeEqual(actual, expected);
+391|}
+392|
+393|function boardToDisplayState(board, winningCells, differenceSet = new 
Set()) {
+394|  return board.map((occupant, index) => {
+395|    const { row, column } = rowColFromIndex(index);
+396|    const labels = [
+397|      `Row ${row}`,
+398|      `Column ${column}`,
+399|      occupant ? `${occupant}` : 'Empty',
+400|    ];
+401|    if (winningCells.includes(index)) labels.push('Winning cell');
+402|    if (differenceSet.has(index)) labels.push('Different cell');
+403|    return {
+404|      occupant,
+405|      row,
+406|      column,
+407|      label: labels.join(', '),
+408|      winning: winningCells.includes(index),
+409|      different: differenceSet.has(index),
+410|    };
+411|  });
+412|}
+413|
+414|function boardToRows(board, winningCells = [], differenceSet = new Set()) {
+415|  const cells = boardToDisplayState(board, winningCells, differenceSet);
+416|  const rows = [];
+417|  for (let row = 1; row <= BOARD_ROWS; row += 1) {
+418|    const rowCells = [];
+419|    for (let column = 1; column <= BOARD_COLS; column += 1) {
+420|      const index = indexFromRowCol(row, column);
+421|      rowCells.push(cells[index]);
+422|    }
+423|    rows.push(rowCells);
+424|  }
+425|  return rows;
+426|}

+436|function buildMoveList(history) {
+437|  return history.map((move, index) => ({
+438|    index: index + 1,
+439|    color: move.color,
+440|    column: move.column,
+441|    row: move.row,
+442|    cellIndex: move.index,
+443|    label: moveLabel(move, index),
+444|  }));

+447|function getCurrentAccount(req) {
+448|  const auth = req.headers.authorization || '';
+449|  const match = auth.match(/^Bearer\s+(.+)$/i);
+450|  if (!match) return null;
+451|  const tokenHash = sha256(match[1]);
+452|  const session = db.prepare('SELECT s.*, a.email, a.name FROM sessions s 
JOIN accounts a ON a.id = s.account_id WHERE s.token_hash = ? AND s.revoked_at 
IS NULL').get(tokenHash);
+453|  if (!session) return null;
+454|  db.prepare('UPDATE sessions SET last_seen_at = ? WHERE id = 
?').run(nowIso(), session.id);
+455|  return {
+456|    id: session.account_id,
+457|    email: session.email,
+458|    name: session.name,
+459|    tokenHash,
+460|  };
+461|}
+462|
+463|function requireAccount(req, res) {
+464|  const account = getCurrentAccount(req);
+465|  if (!account) {
+466|    res.status(401).json(formatUnauthorizedError().body);
+467|    return null;
+468|  }
+469|  return account;

+558|
+559|function getAnalysisById(accountId, analysisId) {
+560|  const analysis = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analyses WHERE account_id = ? 
AND id = ?').get(accountId, analysisId);
+561|  if (!analysis) return null;
+562|  const nodes = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE analysis_id 
= ? ORDER BY id ASC').all(analysisId);
+563|  const serializedNodes = nodes.map((node) => ({
+564|    id: node.id,
+565|    analysisId: node.analysis_id,
+566|    parentId: node.parent_id,
+567|    incomingColumn: node.incoming_column,
+568|    color: node.color,
+569|    row: node.row,
+570|    cellIndex: node.cell_index,
+571|    board: parseBoard(node.board_json),
+572|    turn: node.turn,
+573|    result: node.result,
+574|    winningCells: parseMaybeJson(node.winning_cells_json, []),
+575|    history: parseHistory(node.history_json),
+576|    moveCount: parseHistory(node.history_json).length,
+577|  }));
+578|  const byId = new Map(serializedNodes.map((node) => [node.id, { ...node, 
children: [] }]));
+579|  let root = null;
+580|  for (const node of serializedNodes) {
+581|    const current = byId.get(node.id);
+582|    if (node.parentId === null || node.parentId === undefined) {
+583|      root = current;
+584|    } else {
+585|      const parent = byId.get(node.parentId);
+586|      if (parent) parent.children.push(current);
+587|    }
+588|  }
+589|  const sortTree = (node) => {
+590|    node.children.sort((a, b) => {
+591|      if (a.incomingColumn !== b.incomingColumn) return a.incomingColumn - 
b.incomingColumn;
+592|      return a.id - b.id;
+593|    });
+594|    node.children.forEach(sortTree);
+595|  };
+596|  if (root) sortTree(root);
+597|  return {
+598|    analysis: {
+599|      id: analysis.id,
+600|      accountId: analysis.account_id,
+601|      name: analysis.name,
+602|      sourceMatchId: analysis.source_match_id,
+603|      sourceLabel: analysis.source_label,
+604|      sourceStep: analysis.source_step,
+605|      sourceRoundId: analysis.source_round_id,
+606|      sourcePrefix: parseMaybeJson(analysis.source_prefix_json, []),
+607|      sourceBoard: parseBoard(analysis.source_board_json),
+608|      sourceTurn: analysis.source_turn,
+609|      sourceResult: analysis.source_result,
+610|      sourceWinningCells: 
parseMaybeJson(analysis.source_winning_cells_json, []),
+611|      rootNodeId: analysis.root_node_id,
+612|      selectedNodeId: analysis.selected_node_id,
+613|      revision: analysis.revision,
+614|      updatedAt: analysis.updated_at,
+615|    },
+616|    nodes: serializedNodes,
+617|    tree: root,
+618|  };
+619|}

+621|function nodeLookup(accountId, analysisId, nodeId) {

+623|  if (!analysis) return null;
+624|  const node = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE id = ? AND 
analysis_id = ?').get(nodeId, analysisId);
+625|  if (!node) return null;
+626|  return {
+627|    analysis,
+628|    node: {
+629|      id: node.id,
+630|      analysisId: node.analysis_id,
+631|      parentId: node.parent_id,
+632|      incomingColumn: node.incoming_column,
+633|      color: node.color,
+634|      row: node.row,
+635|      cellIndex: node.cell_index,
+636|      board: parseBoard(node.board_json),
+637|      turn: node.turn,
+638|      result: node.result,
+639|      winningCells: parseMaybeJson(node.winning_cells_json, []),
+640|      history: parseHistory(node.history_json),
+641|    },
+642|  };

+663|function historyPrefixLength(a, b) {
+664|  const limit = Math.min(a.length, b.length);
+665|  let count = 0;
+666|  for (let i = 0; i < limit; i += 1) {
+667|    if (a[i].column !== b[i].column || a[i].color !== b[i].color) break;
+668|    count += 1;
+669|  }
+670|  return count;

+672|
+673|function compareNodes(accountId, analysisId, leftId, rightId) {
+674|  const left = nodeLookup(accountId, analysisId, leftId);
+675|  const right = nodeLookup(accountId, analysisId, rightId);
+676|  if (!left || !right) return null;
+677|  const leftNode = left.node;
+678|  const rightNode = right.node;
+679|  const sameAnalysis = leftNode.analysisId === rightNode.analysisId;
+680|  if (!sameAnalysis) return null;
+681|  const differingCells = [];
+682|  for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_CELLS; i += 1) {
+683|    if (leftNode.board[i] !== rightNode.board[i]) {
+684|      const { row, column } = rowColFromIndex(i);
+685|      differingCells.push({
+686|        index: i,
+687|        row,
+688|        column,
+689|        left: leftNode.board[i] || '',
+690|        right: rightNode.board[i] || '',
+691|      });
+692|    }
+693|  }
+694|  return {
+695|    left: nodeToPublic(leftNode),
+696|    right: nodeToPublic(rightNode),
+697|    commonPrefixMoves: historyPrefixLength(leftNode.history, 
rightNode.history),
+698|    differingCells,
+699|  };
+700|}
+701|
+702|function legalChildrenForNode(node) {
+703|  const board = node.board;
+704|  const result = node.result;
+705|  if (result) return [];
+706|  const children = [];
+707|  for (let column = 1; column <= BOARD_COLS; column += 1) {
+708|    const row = legalDropRow(board, column);
+709|    if (!row) continue;
+710|    const applied = applyMove(board, node.history, column);
+711|    if (applied.error) continue;
+712|    children.push({
+713|      column,
+714|      board: applied.board,
+715|      move: applied.move,
+716|      result: applied.result,
+717|      winningCells: applied.winningCells,
+718|      turn: applied.turn,
+719|    });
+720|  }
+721|  return children;
+722|}
+723|
+724|function evaluatePosition(board, turn, depth, memo) {
+725|  const key = `${board.join('')}|${turn}|${depth}`;
+726|  if (memo.has(key)) return memo.get(key);
+727|  const terminal = boardResult(board);
+728|  if (terminal.result) {
+729|    const winner = terminal.result === 'Draw' ? null : 
terminal.result.split(' ')[0];
+730|    const outcome = winner ? (winner === turn ? 'loss' : 'win') : 'draw';
+731|    const value = { outcome, distance: 0, terminal: true, result: 
terminal.result };
+732|    memo.set(key, value);
+733|    return value;
+734|  }
+735|  if (depth === 0) {
+736|    const value = { outcome: 'unknown', distance: null, terminal: false, 
result: null };
+737|    memo.set(key, value);
+738|    return value;
+739|  }
+740|  const nextTurn = turn === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red';
+741|  let winningDistances = [];
+742|  let losingDistances = [];
+743|  let drawSeen = false;
+744|  let unknownSeen = false;
+745|  const columns = [];
+746|  for (let column = 1; column <= BOARD_COLS; column += 1) {
+747|    const row = legalDropRow(board, column);
+748|    if (!row) continue;
+749|    const applied = applyMove(board, [], column, turn);
+750|    if (applied.error) continue;
+751|    const childEval = evaluatePosition(applied.board, nextTurn, depth - 1, 
memo);
+752|    columns.push({ column, row, childEval });
+753|    if (childEval.outcome === 'loss') {
+754|      winningDistances.push(childEval.distance + 1);
+755|    } else if (childEval.outcome === 'win') {
+756|      losingDistances.push(childEval.distance + 1);
+757|    } else if (childEval.outcome === 'draw') {
+758|      drawSeen = true;
+759|    } else {
+760|      unknownSeen = true;
+761|    }
+762|  }
+763|  let outcome = 'unknown';
+764|  let distance = null;
+765|  if (winningDistances.length) {
+766|    outcome = 'win';
+767|    distance = Math.min(...winningDistances);
+768|  } else if (columns.length && losingDistances.length === columns.length) {
+769|    outcome = 'loss';
+770|    distance = Math.max(...losingDistances);
+771|  } else if (!winningDistances.length && !unknownSeen && drawSeen) {
+772|    outcome = 'draw';
+773|  }
+774|  const value = { outcome, distance, terminal: false, result: null };
+775|  memo.set(key, value);
+776|  return value;
+777|}
+778|
+779|function reportTreeForNode(node, depth, memo, path = []) {
+780|  const terminal = boardResult(node.board);
+781|  const evalResult = evaluatePosition(node.board, node.turn, depth, memo);
+782|  const current = {
+783|    path: path.slice(),
+784|    player: node.turn,
+785|    outcome: evalResult.outcome,
+786|    distance: evalResult.distance,
+787|    result: terminal.result,
+788|    winningCells: node.winningCells,
+789|    board: boardSummary(node.board),
+790|    move: node.incomingColumn ? { column: node.incomingColumn, color: 
node.color } : null,
+791|    terminal: Boolean(terminal.result),
+792|    horizon: !terminal.result && depth === 0,
+793|    children: [],
+794|  };
+795|  if (terminal.result || depth === 0) {
+796|    return current;
+797|  }
+798|  for (let column = 1; column <= BOARD_COLS; column += 1) {
+799|    const row = legalDropRow(node.board, column);
+800|    if (!row) continue;
+801|    const applied = applyMove(node.board, node.history, column);
+802|    if (applied.error) continue;
+803|    const childNode = {
+804|      id: null,
+805|      analysisId: node.analysisId,
+806|      parentId: node.id,
+807|      incomingColumn: column,
+808|      color: node.turn,
+809|      row: applied.move.row,
+810|      cellIndex: applied.move.index,
+811|      board: applied.board,
+812|      turn: applied.turn,
+813|      result: applied.result,
+814|      winningCells: applied.winningCells,
+815|      history: node.history.concat([applied.move]),
+816|    };
+817|    current.children.push(reportTreeForNode(childNode, depth - 1, memo, 
path.concat(column)));
+818|  }
+819|  return current;
+820|}

+845|function createNodeForMove(analysisId, parentNode, column, color, board,

+883|function buildTreeIndex(root) {
+884|  const map = new Map();
+885|  const walk = (node) => {
+886|    map.set(node.id, node);
+887|    for (const child of node.children || []) walk(child);
+888|  };
+889|  if (root) walk(root);
+890|  return map;

+893|function seedDatabase() {
+894|  const accountCount = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM 
accounts').get().count;
+895|  if (accountCount > 0) return;
+896|  if (!fs.existsSync(SEED_XLSX)) {
+897|    throw new Error(`Seed workbook not found at ${SEED_XLSX}`);
+898|  }
+899|  const workbook = xlsx.readFile(SEED_XLSX);
+900|  const accountsSheet = 
xlsx.utils.sheet_to_json(workbook.Sheets['Accounts'], { header: 1, raw: true, 
defval: null });
+901|  const initialSheet = xlsx.utils.sheet_to_json(workbook.Sheets['Initial 
Game State'], { header: 1, raw: true, defval: null });
+902|  const completedSheet = 
xlsx.utils.sheet_to_json(workbook.Sheets['Completed Matches'], { header: 1, raw:
true, defval: null });
+903|
+904|  const accountStmt = db.prepare('INSERT INTO accounts (email, 
password_salt, password_hash, name) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)');
+905|  const stateStmt = db.prepare(`
+906|    INSERT INTO competitive_state (
+907|      account_id, board_json, current_player, status, result, 
winning_cells_json,
+908|      red_wins, yellow_wins, draws, applied_history_json, 
redo_history_json, revision, round_id, updated_at
+909|    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+910|  `);
+911|  const archiveStmt = db.prepare(`
+912|    INSERT INTO competitive_archive (
+913|      match_id, account_id, result, final_board_json, moves_json, 
winning_cells_json, completed_at, round_id
+914|    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+915|  `);
+916|
+917|  const accountRows = accountsSheet.slice(1).filter((row) => row[0]);
+918|  const insertedAccounts = new Map();
+919|  const insertAccountsTx = db.transaction(() => {
+920|    for (const row of accountRows) {
+921|      const email = String(row[0]).trim();
+922|      const password = String(row[1]);
+923|      const name = String(row[2]).trim();
+924|      const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
+925|      const hash = hashPassword(password, salt);
+926|      const info = accountStmt.run(email, salt, hash, name);
+927|      insertedAccounts.set(email, info.lastInsertRowid);
+928|    }
+929|  });
+930|  insertAccountsTx();
+931|
+932|  const initialRows = initialSheet.slice(1).filter((row) => row[0]);
+933|  const insertStateTx = db.transaction(() => {
+934|    for (const row of initialRows) {
+935|      const email = String(row[0]).trim();
+936|      const accountId = insertedAccounts.get(email);
+937|      if (!accountId) continue;
+938|      stateStmt.run(
+939|        accountId,
+940|        JSON.stringify(parseBoard(row[1])),
+941|        row[2],
+942|        row[3],
+943|        row[3] === 'active' ? null : row[3],
+944|        JSON.stringify(row[4] === 'none' ? [] : parseMaybeJson(row[4], 
[])),
+945|        Number(row[5] || 0),
+946|        Number(row[6] || 0),
+947|        Number(row[7] || 0),
+948|        JSON.stringify(row[8] === 'none' ? [] : parseMaybeJson(row[8], 
[])),
+949|        JSON.stringify(row[9] === 'none' ? [] : parseMaybeJson(row[9], 
[])),
+950|        Number(row[10] || 0),
+951|        String(row[11]),
+952|        nowIso()
+953|      );
+954|    }
+955|  });
+956|  insertStateTx();
+957|
+958|  const completedRows = completedSheet.slice(1).filter((row) => row[0]);
+959|  const insertArchiveTx = db.transaction(() => {
+960|    for (const row of completedRows) {
+961|      const email = String(row[0]).trim();
+962|      const accountId = insertedAccounts.get(email);
+963|      if (!accountId) continue;
+964|      const result = String(row[2]);
+965|      const finalBoard = parseBoard(row[3]);
+966|      const moves = parseMaybeJson(row[4], []);
+967|      const winningCells = result === 'Draw' ? [] : 
winningCellsForBoard(finalBoard);
+968|      archiveStmt.run(
+969|        String(row[1]),
+970|        accountId,
+971|        result,
+972|        JSON.stringify(finalBoard),
+973|        JSON.stringify(moves),
+974|        JSON.stringify(winningCells),
+975|        String(row[5]),
+976|        String(row[1])
+977|      );
+978|    }
+979|  });
+980|  insertArchiveTx();
+981|}

+985|    return 'Request contains unsupported fields or is not a JSON object.';
+986|  }

+994|function recordOperation(accountId, opId, requestHash, result) {
+995|  db.prepare('INSERT INTO operations (account_id, op_id, request_hash, 
status_code, response_json, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)').run(
+996|    accountId,
+997|    opId,
+998|    requestHash,
+999|    result.statusCode,
+1000|    JSON.stringify(result.body),
+1001|    nowIso()
+1002|  );
+1003|}
+1004|
+1005|function runOperation(accountId, opId, actionName, requestBody, handler) {
+1006|  const requestHash = sha256(stableStringify({ actionName, requestBody: 
jsonClone(requestBody) }));
+1007|  const existing = operationReceipt(accountId, opId);
+1008|  if (existing) {
+1009|    if (existing.request_hash !== requestHash) {
+1010|      return { statusCode: 409, body: { error: 'Operation identifier 
already used for different input.' } };
+1011|    }
+1012|    return { statusCode: existing.status_code, body: 
JSON.parse(existing.response_json) };
+1013|  }
+1014|  const tx = db.transaction(() => {
+1015|    const result = handler();
+1016|    recordOperation(accountId, opId, requestHash, result);
+1017|    return result;
+1018|  });
+1019|  return tx();

+1067|function createAnalysisFromMatch(accountId, payload) {
+1068|  const { sourceMatchId, sourceStep, name } = payload;
+1069|  const cleanName = validateName(name);
+1070|  if (!cleanName) return formatValidationError('Analysis name must be 1 to
60 trimmed characters long.');
+1071|  if (!Number.isInteger(sourceStep) || sourceStep < 0) return 
formatValidationError('Source step must be an integer from 0 upward.');
+1072|  const match = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM competitive_archive WHERE 
account_id = ? AND match_id = ?').get(accountId, sourceMatchId);
+1073|  if (!match) return formatNotFoundError('Source match not found.');
+1074|  const moves = parseHistory(match.moves_json);
+1075|  if (sourceStep > moves.length) {
+1076|    return formatValidationError('Source step is outside the replay 
range.');
+1077|  }
+1078|  const prefix = moves.slice(0, sourceStep);
+1079|  const state = deriveReplayState(prefix);
+1080|  const tx = db.transaction(() => {
+1081|    const analysisInfo = db.prepare(`
+1082|      INSERT INTO analyses (
+1083|        account_id, name, source_match_id, source_label, source_step, 
source_round_id,
+1084|        source_prefix_json, source_board_json, source_turn, source_result,
source_winning_cells_json,
+1085|        revision, created_at, updated_at
+1086|      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?, ?)
+1087|    `).run(
+1088|      accountId,
+1089|      cleanName,
+1090|      match.match_id,
+1091|      match.match_id,
+1092|      sourceStep,
+1093|      match.round_id,
+1094|      JSON.stringify(prefix),
+1095|      JSON.stringify(state.board),
+1096|      state.turn,
+1097|      state.result,
+1098|      JSON.stringify(state.winningCells),
+1099|      nowIso(),
+1100|      nowIso()
+1101|    );
+1102|    const analysisId = analysisInfo.lastInsertRowid;
+1103|    const rootNodeInfo = db.prepare(`
+1104|      INSERT INTO analysis_nodes (
+1105|        analysis_id, parent_id, incoming_column, color, row, cell_index, 
board_json, turn, result, winning_cells_json, history_json
+1106|      ) VALUES (?, NULL, NULL, ?, NULL, NULL, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+1107|    `).run(
+1108|      analysisId,
+1109|      state.turn === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red',
+1110|      JSON.stringify(state.board),
+1111|      state.turn,
+1112|      state.result,
+1113|      JSON.stringify(state.winningCells),
+1114|      JSON.stringify(prefix)
+1115|    );
+1116|    db.prepare('UPDATE analyses SET root_node_id = ?, selected_node_id = 
?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?').run(rootNodeInfo.lastInsertRowid, 
rootNodeInfo.lastInsertRowid, nowIso(), analysisId);
+1117|    return buildAnalysisResponse(analysisId, accountId);
+1118|  });
+1119|  return tx();

+1122|function deriveReplayState(history) {
+1123|  let board = createEmptyBoard();
+1124|  let result = null;
+1125|  let winningCells = [];
+1126|  for (const move of history) {
+1127|    const applied = applyMove(board, history.slice(0, 0), move.column, 
move.color);
+1128|    if (applied.error) {
+1129|      throw new Error(applied.error);
+1130|    }
+1131|    board = applied.board;
+1132|    result = applied.result;
+1133|    winningCells = applied.winningCells;
+1134|  }
+1135|  return {
+1136|    board,
+1137|    turn: currentPlayerAfterHistory(history),
+1138|    result,
+1139|    winningCells,
+1140|  };

+1158|function previewBranch(accountId, payload) {
+1159|  const allowedKeys = ['opId', 'sourceAnalysisId', 'sourceNodeId', 
'destinationAnalysisId', 'destinationNodeId'];
+1160|  const validation = validateKeysAndValues(payload, allowedKeys);
+1161|  if (validation) return formatValidationError(validation);
+1162|  const { sourceAnalysisId, sourceNodeId, destinationAnalysisId, 
destinationNodeId } = payload;
+1163|  if (![sourceAnalysisId, sourceNodeId, destinationAnalysisId, 
destinationNodeId].every((value) => Number.isInteger(value) && value > 0)) {
+1164|    return formatValidationError('Analysis and node identifiers must be 
positive integers.');
+1165|  }
+1166|  const source = getNodeAndAnalysisOr404(accountId, sourceAnalysisId, 
sourceNodeId);
+1167|  const destination = getNodeAndAnalysisOr404(accountId, 
destinationAnalysisId, destinationNodeId);
+1168|  if (!source || !destination) return formatNotFoundError('Source or 
destination node not found.');
+1169|  if (source.node.parentId === null) return formatValidationError('Source 
branch must be a non-root node.');
+1170|  const opId = payload.opId;
+1171|  if (!validateOpId(opId)) return formatValidationError('Operation 
identifier is missing or malformed.');
+1172|  return runOperation(accountId, opId, 'previewBranch', payload, () => {
+1173|    const sourceAnalysis = getAnalysisById(accountId, sourceAnalysisId);
+1174|    const destinationAnalysis = getAnalysisById(accountId, 
destinationAnalysisId);
+1175|    if (!sourceAnalysis || !destinationAnalysis) return 
formatNotFoundError('Source or destination analysis not found.');
+1176|    const stale = sourceAnalysis.analysis.revision !== 
sourceAnalysis.analysis.revision || destinationAnalysis.analysis.revision !== 
destinationAnalysis.analysis.revision;
+1177|    if (stale) return formatConflictError('Analysis revision is stale.');
+1178|    const previewId = randomId();
+1179|    const result = buildBranchPreview(sourceAnalysis, source.node, 
destinationAnalysis, destination.node);
+1180|    const previewRow = {
+1181|      id: previewId,
+1182|      accountId,
+1183|      sourceAnalysisId,
+1184|      sourceNodeId,
+1185|      destinationAnalysisId,
+1186|      destinationNodeId,
+1187|      sourceRevision: sourceAnalysis.analysis.revision,
+1188|      destinationRevision: destinationAnalysis.analysis.revision,
+1189|      preview: result,
+1190|      committed: 0,
+1191|    };
+1192|    db.prepare(`
+1193|      INSERT INTO previews (
+1194|        id, account_id, source_analysis_id, source_node_id, 
destination_analysis_id, destination_node_id,
+1195|        source_revision, destination_revision, preview_json, committed, 
created_at
+1196|      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?)
+1197|    `).run(
+1198|      previewRow.id,
+1199|      previewRow.accountId,
+1200|      previewRow.sourceAnalysisId,
+1201|      previewRow.sourceNodeId,
+1202|      previewRow.destinationAnalysisId,
+1203|      previewRow.destinationNodeId,
+1204|      previewRow.sourceRevision,
+1205|      previewRow.destinationRevision,
+1206|      JSON.stringify(result),
+1207|      nowIso()
+1208|    );
+1209|    return {
+1210|      statusCode: 200,
+1211|      body: {
+1212|        previewId,
+1213|        sourceAnalysisId,
+1214|        sourceNodeId,
+1215|        destinationAnalysisId,
+1216|        destinationNodeId,
+1217|        sourceRevision: sourceAnalysis.analysis.revision,
+1218|        destinationRevision: destinationAnalysis.analysis.revision,
+1219|        ...result,
+1220|      },
+1221|    };
+1222|  });

+1225|function cloneMove(move) {
+1226|  return {
+1227|    color: move.color,
+1228|    column: move.column,
+1229|    row: move.row,
+1230|    index: move.index,

+1234|function buildBranchPreview(sourceAnalysis, sourceNode, 
destinationAnalysis, destinationNode) {
+1235|  const sourceIndex = sourceNode.history.length - 
sourceAnalysis.analysis.sourcePrefix.length;
+1236|  const sourceBranchRootHistory = sourceNode.history.slice();
+1237|  const destinationPathHistory = destinationNode.history.slice();
+1238|  const mappingPreview = [];
+1239|  let newCount = 0;
+1240|  let reusedCount = 0;
+1241|  let illegal = null;
+1242|
+1243|  function walk(sourceCurrent, destinationCurrent, relativeMoves) {
+1244|    const nextRelativeMoves = sourceCurrent.parentId === null ? 
relativeMoves : relativeMoves.concat([sourceCurrent.incomingColumn]);
+1245|    if (sourceCurrent.id !== sourceNode.id) {
+1246|      const existingChild = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE
analysis_id = ? AND parent_id = ? AND incoming_column = 
?').get(destinationCurrent.analysisId, destinationCurrent.id, 
sourceCurrent.incomingColumn);
+1247|      if (existingChild) {
+1248|        reusedCount += 1;
+1249|        mappingPreview.push({
+1250|          sourceNodeId: sourceCurrent.id,
+1251|          destinationNodeId: existingChild.id,
+1252|          relativePath: nextRelativeMoves,
+1253|          board: parseBoard(existingChild.board_json),
+1254|          result: existingChild.result,
+1255|          reused: true,
+1256|        });
+1257|        for (const child of db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE
parent_id = ? ORDER BY incoming_column ASC, id ASC').all(sourceCurrent.id)) {
+1258|          walk(child, existingChild, nextRelativeMoves);
+1259|          if (illegal) return;
+1260|        }
+1261|        return;
+1262|      }
+1263|      const applied = applyMove(parseBoard(destinationCurrent.board_json),
parseHistory(destinationCurrent.history_json), sourceCurrent.incomingColumn, 
destinationCurrent.turn);
+1264|      if (applied.error) {
+1265|        illegal = {
+1266|          path: nextRelativeMoves,
+1267|          reason: applied.error,
+1268|        };
+1269|        return;
+1270|      }
+1271|      const newNode = {
+1272|        id: null,
+1273|        analysisId: destinationCurrent.analysisId,
+1274|        parentId: destinationCurrent.id,
+1275|        incomingColumn: sourceCurrent.incomingColumn,
+1276|        color: destinationCurrent.turn,
+1277|        row: applied.move.row,
+1278|        cellIndex: applied.move.index,
+1279|        board: applied.board,
+1280|        turn: applied.turn,
+1281|        result: applied.result,
+1282|        winningCells: applied.winningCells,
+1283|        history: 
parseHistory(destinationCurrent.history_json).concat([applied.move]),
+1284|      };
+1285|      newCount += 1;
+1286|      mappingPreview.push({
+1287|        sourceNodeId: sourceCurrent.id,
+1288|        destinationNodeId: null,
+1289|        relativePath: nextRelativeMoves,
+1290|        board: boardSummary(newNode.board),
+1291|        result: newNode.result,
+1292|        reused: false,
+1293|      });
+1294|      for (const child of db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE 
parent_id = ? ORDER BY incoming_column ASC, id ASC').all(sourceCurrent.id)) {
+1295|        walk(child, { ...destinationCurrent, id: null, board_json: 
JSON.stringify(newNode.board), history_json: JSON.stringify(newNode.history), 
turn: newNode.turn, analysisId: destinationCurrent.analysisId }, 
nextRelativeMoves);
+1296|        if (illegal) return;
+1297|      }
+1298|    }
+1299|  }
+1300|
+1301|  const sourceChildren = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE 
parent_id = ? ORDER BY incoming_column ASC, id ASC').all(sourceNode.id);
+1302|  for (const child of sourceChildren) {
+1303|    walk(child, destinationNode, []);
+1304|    if (illegal) break;
+1305|  }
+1306|  return {
+1307|    preview: {
+1308|      sourceBranchRootNodeId: sourceNode.id,
+1309|      sourceBranchRootMove: sourceNode.incomingColumn,
+1310|      destinationNodeId: destinationNode.id,
+1311|      relativePaths: mappingPreview,
+1312|      newCount,
+1313|      reusedCount,
+1314|      illegal,
+1315|      sourceBranchLength: sourceNode.history.length - 
sourceAnalysis.analysis.sourcePrefix.length,
+1316|      destinationMoveCount: destinationNode.history.length,
+1317|    },
+1318|  };
+1319|}

+1321|function commitBranch(accountId, payload) {
+1322|  const allowedKeys = ['opId', 'previewId'];
+1323|  const validation = validateKeysAndValues(payload, allowedKeys);
+1324|  if (validation) return formatValidationError(validation);
+1325|  const { previewId, opId } = payload;
+1326|  if (typeof previewId !== 'string' || !previewId.trim()) return 
formatValidationError('Preview identifier is required.');
+1327|  if (!validateOpId(opId)) return formatValidationError('Operation 
identifier is missing or malformed.');
+1328|  return runOperation(accountId, opId, 'commitBranch', payload, () => {
+1329|    const preview = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM previews WHERE account_id = 
? AND id = ?').get(accountId, previewId);
+1330|    if (!preview) return formatNotFoundError('Preview not found.');
+1331|    if (preview.committed) {
+1332|      return formatConflictError('Preview has already been committed.', { 
previewId });
+1333|    }
+1334|    const sourceAnalysis = getAnalysisById(accountId, 
preview.source_analysis_id);
+1335|    const destinationAnalysis = getAnalysisById(accountId, 
preview.destination_analysis_id);
+1336|    if (!sourceAnalysis || !destinationAnalysis) return 
formatNotFoundError('Source or destination analysis not found.');
+1337|    if (sourceAnalysis.analysis.revision !== preview.source_revision || 
destinationAnalysis.analysis.revision !== preview.destination_revision) {
+1338|      return formatConflictError('Preview is stale and must be 
regenerated.', {
+1339|        analysis: destinationAnalysis.analysis,
+1340|        nodes: destinationAnalysis.nodes.map(nodeToPublic),
+1341|      });
+1342|    }
+1343|    const previewData = JSON.parse(preview.preview_json);
+1344|    if (previewData.illegal) {
+1345|      return formatConflictError('Preview contains illegal moves and 
cannot be committed.', previewData.illegal);
+1346|    }
+1347|    const commitResult = commitBranchPreview(preview, sourceAnalysis, 
destinationAnalysis);
+1348|    return {
+1349|      statusCode: 200,
+1350|      body: commitResult,
+1351|    };
+1352|  });

+1354|
+1355|function commitBranchPreview(preview, sourceAnalysis, destinationAnalysis)
{
+1356|  const sourceRoot = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE id = ?
AND analysis_id = ?').get(preview.source_node_id, preview.source_analysis_id);
+1357|  const destinationRoot = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE 
id = ? AND analysis_id = ?').get(preview.destination_node_id, 
preview.destination_analysis_id);
+1358|  const sourceChildren = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE 
parent_id = ? ORDER BY incoming_column ASC, id ASC').all(sourceRoot.id);
+1359|  const mapping = [];
+1360|  let newCount = 0;
+1361|  let reusedCount = 0;
+1362|
+1363|  const nodeCache = new Map();
+1364|  const ensureCommit = (sourceNodeId, destinationParentId) => {
+1365|    const sourceNode = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE id =
? AND analysis_id = ?').get(sourceNodeId, preview.source_analysis_id);
+1366|    if (!sourceNode) return null;
+1367|    const sourceChildrenRows = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes 
WHERE parent_id = ? ORDER BY incoming_column ASC, id ASC').all(sourceNode.id);
+1368|    if (sourceNode.id === sourceRoot.id) {
+1369|      for (const child of sourceChildrenRows) {
+1370|        ensureCommit(child.id, destinationRoot.id);
+1371|      }
+1372|      return destinationRoot.id;
+1373|    }
+1374|    const destinationParent = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes 
WHERE id = ? AND analysis_id = ?').get(destinationParentId, 
preview.destination_analysis_id);
+1375|    if (!destinationParent) return null;
+1376|    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM analysis_nodes WHERE 
analysis_id = ? AND parent_id = ? AND incoming_column = 
?').get(preview.destination_analysis_id, destinationParent.id, 
sourceNode.incoming_column);
+1377|    if (existing) {
+1378|      reusedCount += 1;
+1379|      mapping.push({ sourceNodeId: sourceNode.id, destinationNodeId: 
existing.id, reused: true });
+1380|      for (const child of sourceChildrenRows) {
+1381|        ensureCommit(child.id, existing.id);
+1382|      }
+1383|      return existing.id;
+1384|    }
+1385|    const parentBoard = parseBoard(destinationParent.board_json);
+1386|    const parentHistory = parseHistory(destinationParent.history_json);
+1387|    const applied = applyMove(parentBoard, parentHistory, 
sourceNode.incoming_column, destinationParent.turn);
+1388|    if (applied.error) {
+1389|      return null;
+1390|    }
+1391|    const inserted = db.prepare(`
+1392|      INSERT INTO analysis_nodes (
+1393|        analysis_id, parent_id, incoming_column, color, row, cell_index, 
board_json, turn, result, winning_cells_json, history_json
+1394|      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+1395|    `).run(
+1396|      preview.destination_analysis_id,
+1397|      destinationParent.id,
+1398|      sourceNode.incoming_column,
+1399|      destinationParent.turn,
+1400|      applied.move.row,
+1401|      applied.move.index,
+1402|      JSON.stringify(applied.board),
+1403|      applied.turn,
+1404|      applied.result,
+1405|      JSON.stringify(applied.winningCells),
+1406|      JSON.stringify(parentHistory.concat([applied.move]))
+1407|    );
+1408|    newCount += 1;
+1409|    const newId = inserted.lastInsertRowid;
+1410|    mapping.push({ sourceNodeId: sourceNode.id, destinationNodeId: newId, 
reused: false });
+1411|    for (const child of sourceChildrenRows) {
+1412|      ensureCommit(child.id, newId);
+1413|    }
+1414|    return newId;
+1415|  };
+1416|
+1417|  for (const child of sourceChildren) {
+1418|    ensureCommit(child.id, destinationRoot.id);
+1419|  }
+1420|
+1421|  db.prepare('UPDATE analyses SET revision = revision + 1, updated_at = ? 
WHERE id = ?').run(nowIso(), preview.destination_analysis_id);
+1422|  const latest = buildAnalysisResponse(preview.destination_analysis_id, 
sourceAnalysis.analysis.accountId);
+1423|  db.prepare('UPDATE previews SET committed = 1, committed_at = ? WHERE id
= ?').run(nowIso(), preview.id);
+1424|  return {
+1425|    previewId: preview.id,
+1426|    sourceAnalysisId: preview.source_analysis_id,
+1427|    destinationAnalysisId: preview.destination_analysis_id,
+1428|    newCount,
+1429|    reusedCount,
+1430|    mapping,
+1431|    analysis: latest.analysis,
+1432|    nodes: latest.nodes,
+1433|    tree: latest.tree,
+1434|  };

+1470|function maybeUpdateArchiveForCurrentState(accountId, state) {
+1471|  const existing = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM competitive_archive WHERE

+1472|  if (state.result && state.result !== 'Draw' && 
state.result.endsWith('wins') || state.result === 'Draw') {
+1473|    if (!existing) {
+1474|      db.prepare(`
+1475|        INSERT INTO competitive_archive (match_id, account_id, result, 
final_board_json, moves_json, winning_cells_json, completed_at, round_id)
+1476|        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+1477|      `).run(
+1478|        state.roundId,
+1479|        accountId,
+1480|        state.result,
+1481|        JSON.stringify(state.board),
+1482|        JSON.stringify(state.history),
+1483|        JSON.stringify(state.winningCells),
+1484|        nowIso(),
+1485|        state.roundId
+1486|      );
+1487|    } else {
+1488|      db.prepare(`
+1489|        UPDATE competitive_archive SET result = ?, final_board_json = ?, 
moves_json = ?, winning_cells_json = ?, completed_at = ? WHERE account_id = ? 
AND round_id = ?
+1490|      `).run(state.result, JSON.stringify(state.board), 
JSON.stringify(state.history), JSON.stringify(state.winningCells), nowIso(), 
accountId, state.roundId);
+1491|    }
+1492|  } else if (existing) {
+1493|    db.prepare('DELETE FROM competitive_archive WHERE account_id = ? AND 
round_id = ?').run(accountId, state.roundId);
+1494|  }

+1497|function applyCompetitiveMove(accountId, payload) {
+1498|  const allowedKeys = ['opId', 'expectedRevision', 'column'];
+1499|  const validation = validateKeysAndValues(payload, allowedKeys);
+1500|  if (validation) return formatValidationError(validation);
+1501|  const { opId, expectedRevision, column } = payload;
+1502|  if (!validateOpId(opId)) return formatValidationError('Operation 
identifier is missing or malformed.');
+1503|  if (!Number.isInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) return 
formatValidationError('Expected revision must be a non-negative integer.');
+1504|  if (!Number.isInteger(column) || column < 1 || column > BOARD_COLS) 
return formatValidationError('Column must be an integer from 1 to 7.');
+1505|  return runOperation(accountId, opId, 'competitiveMove', payload, () => {
+1506|    const current = getCompetitiveState(accountId);
+1507|    if (!current) return formatNotFoundError('Competitive game not 
found.');
+1508|    if (current.revision !== expectedRevision) {
+1509|      return formatConflictError('Competitive game revision is stale.', { 
game: serializeCompetitiveState(current) });
+1510|    }
+1511|    const applied = applyMove(current.board, current.history, column);
+1512|    if (applied.error) return formatValidationError(applied.error);
+1513|    current.board = applied.board;
+1514|    current.history = current.history.concat([applied.move]);
+1515|    current.redoHistory = [];
+1516|    current.currentPlayer = applied.turn;
+1517|    current.result = applied.result;
+1518|    current.winningCells = applied.winningCells;
+1519|    current.revision += 1;
+1520|    current.status = applied.result ? 'completed' : 'active';
+1521|    if (applied.result === 'Red wins') current.redWins += 1;
+1522|    if (applied.result === 'Yellow wins') current.yellowWins += 1;
+1523|    if (applied.result === 'Draw') current.draws += 1;
+1524|    maybeUpdateArchiveForCurrentState(accountId, current);
+1525|    updateCompetitiveState(accountId, current);
+1526|    return { statusCode: 200, body: { game: 
serializeCompetitiveState(current) } };
+1527|  });
+1528|}
+1529|
+1530|function undoCompetitiveMove(accountId, payload) {
+1531|  const allowedKeys = ['opId', 'expectedRevision'];
+1532|  const validation = validateKeysAndValues(payload, allowedKeys);
+1533|  if (validation) return formatValidationError(validation);
+1534|  const { opId, expectedRevision } = payload;
+1535|  if (!validateOpId(opId)) return formatValidationError('Operation 
identifier is missing or malformed.');
+1536|  if (!Number.isInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) return 
formatValidationError('Expected revision must be a non-negative integer.');
+1537|  return runOperation(accountId, opId, 'competitiveUndo', payload, () => {
+1538|    const current = getCompetitiveState(accountId);
+1539|    if (!current) return formatNotFoundError('Competitive game not 
found.');
+1540|    if (current.revision !== expectedRevision) {
+1541|      return formatConflictError('Competitive game revision is stale.', { 
game: serializeCompetitiveState(current) });
+1542|    }
+1543|    if (!current.history.length) return formatValidationError('Undo is not
available at the root.');
+1544|    const lastMove = current.history[current.history.length - 1];
+1545|    const nextBoard = cloneBoard(current.board);
+1546|    nextBoard[lastMove.index] = '';
+1547|    current.board = nextBoard;
+1548|    current.history = current.history.slice(0, -1);
+1549|    current.redoHistory = [lastMove].concat(current.redoHistory);
+1550|    current.currentPlayer = lastMove.color;
+1551|    current.result = null;
+1552|    current.winningCells = [];
+1553|    current.revision += 1;
+1554|    current.status = 'active';
+1555|    const derived = boardResult(current.board);
+1556|    if (derived.result) {
+1557|      current.result = derived.result;
+1558|      current.winningCells = derived.winningCells;
+1559|      current.status = 'completed';
+1560|    }
+1561|    if (lastMove.color === 'Red' && current.redWins > 0 && current.result 
!== 'Red wins') {
+1562|      current.redWins -= 1;
+1563|    }
+1564|    if (lastMove.color === 'Yellow' && current.yellowWins > 0 && 
current.result !== 'Yellow wins') {
+1565|      current.yellowWins -= 1;
+1566|    }
+1567|    if (lastMove.color && current.result !== 'Draw' && current.draws > 0) 
{
+1568|      if (lastMove.color === 'Red' || lastMove.color === 'Yellow') {
+1569|        const archive = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM competitive_archive 
WHERE account_id = ? AND round_id = ?').get(accountId, current.roundId);
+1570|        if (archive && archive.result === 'Draw') {
+1571|          current.draws -= 1;
+1572|        }
+1573|      }
+1574|    }
+1575|    maybeUpdateArchiveForCurrentState(accountId, current);
+1576|    updateCompetitiveState(accountId, current);
+1577|    return { statusCode: 200, body: { game: 
serializeCompetitiveState(current) } };
+1578|  });
+1579|}
+1580|
+1581|function redoCompetitiveMove(accountId, payload) {
+1582|  const allowedKeys = ['opId', 'expectedRevision'];
+1583|  const validation = validateKeysAndValues(payload, allowedKeys);
+1584|  if (validation) return formatValidationError(validation);
+1585|  const { opId, expectedRevision } = payload;
+1586|  if (!validateOpId(opId)) return formatValidationError('Operation 
identifier is missing or malformed.');
+1587|  if (!Number.isInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) return 
formatValidationError('Expected revision must be a non-negative integer.');
+1588|  return runOperation(accountId, opId, 'competitiveRedo', payload, () => {
+1589|    const current = getCompetitiveState(accountId);
+1590|    if (!current) return formatNotFoundError('Competitive game not

+1591|    if (current.revision !== expectedRevision) {
+1592|      return formatConflictError('Competitive game revision is stale.', { 
game: serializeCompetitiveState(current) });
+1593|    }
+1594|    if (!current.redoHistory.length) return formatValidationError('Redo is
not available.');
+1595|    const redoMove = current.redoHistory[0];
+1596|    const rest = current.redoHistory.slice(1);
+1597|    const applied = applyMove(current.board, current.history,

+1598|    if (applied.error) return formatValidationError(applied.error);
+1599|    current.board = applied.board;
+1600|    current.history = current.history.concat([applied.move]);
+1601|    current.redoHistory = rest;
+1602|    current.currentPlayer = applied.turn;
+1603|    current.result = applied.result;
+1604|    current.winningCells = applied.winningCells;
+1605|    current.revision += 1;
+1606|    current.status = applied.result ? 'completed' : 'active';
+1607|    if (applied.result === 'Red wins') current.redWins += 1;
+1608|    if (applied.result === 'Yellow wins') current.yellowWins += 1;
+1609|    if (applied.result === 'Draw') current.draws += 1;
+1610|    maybeUpdateArchiveForCurrentState(accountId, current);
+1611|    updateCompetitiveState(accountId, current);
+1612|    return { statusCode: 200, body: { game: 
serializeCompetitiveState(current) } };
+1613|  });

+1644|function applyAnalysisSelect(accountId, analysisId, payload) {

+1650|  if (!Number.isInteger(expectedRevision) || expectedRevision < 0) return
