+152|function moveLabel(move) {
+153|  return `${move.index}. ${move.color} column ${move.column} → row 
${move.row}`;
+154|}
+155|
+156|function nodeLabel(node) {
+157|  if (node.parentId == null) return `Root · ${node.turn || '—'} to move`;
+158|  const prefix = node.history?.length ? `${node.history.length}. ` : '';
+159|  return `${prefix}${node.color} column ${node.incomingColumn} → row 
${node.row}${node.result ? ` · ${node.result}` : ''}`;
+160|}
+161|
+162|function boardLabel(row, column, occupant, winning = false, different = 
false) {
+163|  const parts = [`Row ${row}`, `Column ${column}`, occupant || 'Empty'];
+164|  if (winning) parts.push('Winning cell');
+165|  if (different) parts.push('Different cell');
+166|  return parts.join(', ');
+167|}
+168|
+169|function renderBoard(container, board, options = {}) {
+170|  if (!board || board.length !== TOTAL_CELLS) {
+171|    container.innerHTML = '<p class="muted">No board loaded.</p>';
+172|    return;
+173|  }
+174|  const winning = new Set(options.winningCells || []);
+175|  const diff = new Set(options.differences || []);
+176|  const canPlay = typeof options.onColumn === 'function';
+177|  const title = options.title || '';
+178|  const columnButtons = [];
+179|  for (let col = 1; col <= BOARD_COLS; col += 1) {
+180|    const disabled = options.disabled || !canPlay;
+181|    columnButtons.push(`<button class="secondary column-btn" 
data-column="${col}" type="button" ${disabled ? 'disabled' : ''} 
aria-label="Drop in column ${col}">${col}</button>`);
+182|  }
+183|  const cells = [];
+184|  for (let row = 1; row <= BOARD_ROWS; row += 1) {
+185|    for (let column = 1; column <= BOARD_COLS; column += 1) {
+186|      const index = (row - 1) * BOARD_COLS + (column - 1);
+187|      const occupant = board[index] || '';
+188|      const classes = ['cell'];
+189|      if (occupant === 'Red') classes.push('red');
+190|      if (occupant === 'Yellow') classes.push('yellow');
+191|      if (winning.has(index)) classes.push('win');
+192|      if (diff.has(index)) classes.push('diff');
+193|      cells.push(`<div class="${classes.join(' ')}" role="gridcell" 
aria-label="${escapeHtml(boardLabel(row, column, occupant, winning.has(index), 
diff.has(index)))}"></div>`);
+194|    }
+195|  }
+196|  container.innerHTML = `
+197|    <div class="board-shell">
+198|      ${title ? `<div 
class="board-title"><strong>${escapeHtml(title)}</strong></div>` : ''}
+199|      <div class="column-row">${columnButtons.join('')}</div>
+200|      <div class="board-grid" role="grid" aria-label="Connect Four 
board">${cells.join('')}</div>
+201|    </div>
+202|  `;
+203|  container.querySelectorAll('.column-btn').forEach((button) => {
+204|    button.addEventListener('click', () => 
options.onColumn(Number(button.dataset.column)));
+205|  });
+206|}
+207|
+208|function renderHistory(listEl, history, emptyText = 'No moves yet.') {
+209|  if (!history || !history.length) {
+210|    listEl.innerHTML = `<li class="muted">${escapeHtml(emptyText)}</li>`;
+211|    return;
+212|  }
+213|  listEl.innerHTML = history.map((move) => 
`<li>${escapeHtml(moveLabel(move))}</li>`).join('');
+214|}

${analysis.sourceStep} · revision ${analysis.revision}</div>

data-open-analysis="${analysis.id}">Open</button>
+228|    </div>

els.analysisList.querySelectorAll('[data-open-analysis]').forEach((button) => {

+239|function analysisTreeMarkup(node, selectedId) {
+240|  if (!node) return '<p class="muted">No tree loaded.</p>';
+241|  const selected = node.id === selectedId;
+242|  const children = (node.children || []).map((child) => 
analysisTreeMarkup(child, selectedId)).join('');
+243|  return `
+244|    <div class="tree-node ${selected ? 'selected' : ''}">
+245|      <button class="secondary" type="button" 
data-select-node="${node.id}">${escapeHtml(nodeLabel(node))}</button>
+246|      <div class="node-meta">${escapeHtml(`Node ${node.id} · path 
${node.history && node.history.length ? node.history.map((move) => 
move.column).join(' → ') : 'root'}`)}</div>
+247|      ${children}
+248|    </div>
+249|  `;

+251|
+252|function compareBoardMarkup(board, winningCells = [], diffCells = []) {
+253|  const diffSet = new Set(diffCells.map((cell) => cell.index));
+254|  const winSet = new Set(winningCells || []);
+255|  const cells = [];
+256|  for (let row = 1; row <= BOARD_ROWS; row += 1) {
+257|    for (let column = 1; column <= BOARD_COLS; column += 1) {
+258|      const index = (row - 1) * BOARD_COLS + (column - 1);
+259|      const occupant = board[index] || '';
+260|      const classes = ['cell'];
+261|      if (occupant === 'Red') classes.push('red');
+262|      if (occupant === 'Yellow') classes.push('yellow');
+263|      if (winSet.has(index)) classes.push('win');
+264|      if (diffSet.has(index)) classes.push('diff');
+265|      cells.push(`<div class="${classes.join(' ')}" role="gridcell" 
aria-label="${escapeHtml(boardLabel(row, column, occupant, winSet.has(index), 
diffSet.has(index)))}"></div>`);
+266|    }
+267|  }
+268|  return `<div class="board-grid compare-board" role="grid" 
aria-label="Position comparison board">${cells.join('')}</div>`;
+269|}

+271|function proofTreeMarkup(node, selectedPath = []) {
+272|  if (!node) return '<p class="muted">No report loaded.</p>';
+273|  const pathText = node.path && node.path.length ? node.path.join(' → ') : 
'root';
+274|  const children = (node.children || []).map((child) => 
proofTreeMarkup(child, selectedPath)).join('');
+275|  return `
+276|    <div class="tree-node">
+277|      <button class="secondary" type="button" 
data-proof-path="${escapeHtml(pathText)}" 
data-proof-board="${escapeHtml(JSON.stringify(node.board))}">
+278|        ${escapeHtml(pathText)} · ${escapeHtml(node.player)} · 
${escapeHtml(node.outcome)}${node.distance !== null && node.distance !== 
undefined ? ` in ${node.distance} plies` : ''}${node.terminal ? ' · terminal' : 
''}${node.horizon ? ' · horizon' : ''}
+279|      </button>
+280|      ${children}
+281|    </div>
+282|  `;

+398|async function syncBranchNodeSelectors() {
+399|  const sourceAnalysisId = Number(els.branchSourceAnalysis.value || 
state.analysis?.analysis?.id);
+400|  const destinationAnalysisId = Number(els.branchDestinationAnalysis.value

+402|  const destinationDetail = await 
ensureAnalysisDetail(destinationAnalysisId);
+403|  if (sourceDetail) {

!= null);

'<option value="">No source nodes</option>';

els.branchSourceNode.value = String(sourceNodes[0].id);
+407|  }
+408|  if (destinationDetail) {

destinationDetail.nodes.map((node) => `<option

+410|    if (!els.branchDestinationNode.value && 
destinationDetail.analysis.rootNodeId) els.branchDestinationNode.value =

+414|function renderCompareResult(result) {

+500|function renderButtons() {

+512|
+513|function replayFromMoves(moves) {
+514|  let board = Array.from({ length: TOTAL_CELLS }, () => '');
+515|  let winningCells = [];
+516|  let turn = 'Red';
+517|  let result = null;
+518|  for (let i = 0; i < moves.length; i += 1) {
+519|    const move = moves[i];
+520|    const row = findDropRow(board, move.column);
+521|    if (!row) continue;
+522|    const index = (row - 1) * BOARD_COLS + (move.column - 1);
+523|    board[index] = move.color;
+524|    const win = detectWin(board);
+525|    result = win.result;
+526|    winningCells = win.winningCells;
+527|    turn = move.color === 'Red' ? 'Yellow' : 'Red';
+528|  }
+529|  return { board, winningCells, turn, result };
+530|}
+531|
+532|function findDropRow(board, column) {
+533|  for (let row = BOARD_ROWS; row >= 1; row -= 1) {
+534|    const index = (row - 1) * BOARD_COLS + (column - 1);
+535|    if (!board[index]) return row;
+536|  }
+537|  return null;
+538|}
+539|
+540|function detectWin(board) {
+541|  const dirs = [[0, 1], [1, 0], [1, 1], [1, -1]];
+542|  for (let row = 1; row <= BOARD_ROWS; row += 1) {
+543|    for (let column = 1; column <= BOARD_COLS; column += 1) {
+544|      const occupant = board[(row - 1) * BOARD_COLS + (column - 1)];
+545|      if (!occupant) continue;
+546|      for (const [dr, dc] of dirs) {
+547|        const cells = [[row, column]];
+548|        let ok = true;
+549|        for (let step = 1; step < 4; step += 1) {
+550|          const r = row + dr * step;
+551|          const c = column + dc * step;
+552|          if (r < 1 || r > BOARD_ROWS || c < 1 || c > BOARD_COLS) { ok = 
false; break; }
+553|          const idx = (r - 1) * BOARD_COLS + (c - 1);
+554|          if (board[idx] !== occupant) { ok = false; break; }
+555|          cells.push([r, c]);
+556|        }
+557|        if (ok) return { result: `${occupant} wins`, winningCells: 
cells.map(([r, c]) => (r - 1) * BOARD_COLS + (c - 1)) };
+558|      }
+559|    }
+560|  }
+561|  if (board.every(Boolean)) return { result: 'Draw', winningCells: [] };
+562|  return { result: null, winningCells: [] };
+563|}

+565|async function loadBootstrap() {
+566|  if (!state.token) return null;
+567|  const data = await api('/api/bootstrap');
+568|  state.me = data.account;
+569|  state.bootstrap = data;
+570|  state.game = data.game;
+571|  state.archive = data.archive || [];
+572|  state.analyses = data.analyses || [];
+573|  state.analysisCache.clear();
+574|  if (state.analysis) state.analysisCache.set(state.analysis.analysis.id, 
state.analysis);
+575|  renderSession();
+576|  renderGame();
+577|  renderArchiveList();
+578|  renderAnalysisList();
+579|  await restoreOpenPanels();
+580|  renderButtons();
+581|  return data;
+582|}

+584|async function restoreOpenPanels() {
+585|  const openAnalysisId = 
Number(localStorage.getItem('dropline-open-analysis') || '0');
+586|  if (Number.isInteger(openAnalysisId) && openAnalysisId > 0 && 
state.analyses.some((analysis) => analysis.id === openAnalysisId)) {
+587|    try {
+588|      await openAnalysis(openAnalysisId, { silent: true });
+589|    } catch {
+590|      state.analysis = null;
+591|      localStorage.removeItem('dropline-open-analysis');
+592|    }
+593|  } else if (!state.analysis) {
+594|    state.analysis = null;
+595|  }
+596|  const archiveId = localStorage.getItem('dropline-archive-match');
+597|  if (archiveId && state.archive.some((match) => match.matchId === 
archiveId)) {
+598|    try {
+599|      await loadArchiveDetail(archiveId, { silent: true });
+600|    } catch {
+601|      state.archiveDetail = null;
+602|      localStorage.removeItem('dropline-archive-match');
+603|    }
+604|  } else if (!state.archiveDetail && state.archive.length) {
+605|    await loadArchiveDetail(state.archive[0].matchId, { silent: true });
+606|  }
+607|  renderAnalysis();
+608|  renderArchive();
+609|}

value="${escapeHtml(match.matchId)}">${escapeHtml(match.matchId)} · 
${escapeHtml(match.result)} · ${match.moveCount} moves</option>`).join('');
+613|  if (state.archiveDetail) els.archiveSelect.value = 
state.archiveDetail.matchId;

+616|async function loadArchiveDetail(matchId, options = {}) {
+617|  if (!matchId) return null;
+618|  localStorage.setItem('dropline-archive-match', matchId);
+619|  const data = await api(`/api/archive/${encodeURIComponent(matchId)}`);
+620|  state.archiveDetail = data;
+621|  if (!options.silent) {
+622|    els.archiveStep.value = String(data.moveCount);
+623|  }
+624|  renderArchive();
+625|  return data;

+628|async function openAnalysis(id, options = {}) {

+632|  state.analysis = data;
+633|  state.analysisCache.set(id, data);
+634|  if (!options.silent) {
+635|    els.analysisNameInput.value = data.analysis.name;
+636|  }
+637|  renderAnalysis();
+638|  return data;

+793|  if (!state.analysis || isBusy('analysisRedo')) return;
+794|  setBusy('analysisRedo', true);
+795|  try {
+796|    const body = { opId: makeOpId(), expectedRevision: 
state.analysis.analysis.revision };
+797|    if (!els.analysisRedoChoice.classList.contains('hidden') && 
els.analysisRedoChoice.value) {
+798|      body.childId = Number(els.analysisRedoChoice.value);
+799|    }
+800|    const data = await

});
+801|    state.analysis = data;

+803|    await loadBootstrap();
+804|    setMessage('Selected a continuation.', 'good');
+805|  } catch (error) {
+806|    if (error.status === 409 && error.data?.children) {
+807|      els.analysisRedoChoice.classList.remove('hidden');
+808|      els.analysisRedoChoice.innerHTML = error.data.children.map((child) =>
`<option 
value="${child.id}">${escapeHtml(nodeLabel(child))}</option>`).join('');
+809|      setMessage('Redo is ambiguous. Choose a continuation.', 'warn');
+810|      return;
+811|    }
+812|    handleError(error, 'analysis redo');
+813|  } finally {
+814|    setBusy('analysisRedo', false);
+815|    renderAnalysis();
+816|  }

+1006|  els.gameUndoBtn.addEventListener('click', undoCompetitiveMove);
+1007|  els.gameRedoBtn.addEventListener('click', redoCompetitiveMove);

+1011|  els.createAnalysisFromReplay.addEventListener('click',

+1014|    localStorage.removeItem('dropline-open-analysis');
+1015|    renderAnalysis();
+1016|    setMessage('Returned to the live game.', 'good');
+1017|  });
+1018|  els.renameAnalysisBtn.addEventListener('click', renameAnalysis);
+1019|  els.analysisUndoBtn.addEventListener('click', undoAnalysisMove);
+1020|  els.analysisRedoBtn.addEventListener('click', redoAnalysisMove);
+1021|  els.compareBtn.addEventListener('click', compareAnalysisNodes);

+1023|  els.branchCommitBtn.addEventListener('click', commitBranchCopy);

+1028|  if (state.token) {
+1029|    try {
+1030|      await loadBootstrap();

+1065|    let nextIndex = currentIndex;
+1066|    if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') nextIndex = Math.max(0, currentIndex - 
1);
+1067|    if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') nextIndex = Math.min(buttons.length - 
1, currentIndex + 1);
+1068|    if (event.key === 'Home') nextIndex = 0;
+1069|    if (event.key === 'End') nextIndex = buttons.length - 1;
+1070|    if (nextIndex >= 0 && buttons[nextIndex]) {
+1071|      event.preventDefault();
+1072|      buttons[nextIndex].focus();
+1073|    }
+1074|  }
+1075|  if ((event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') && 
event.target?.tagName === 'BUTTON') {
+1076|    event.preventDefault();
